# Form-to-Table Mapping

Status: **partial** — derived from `rwn_symbols.json` (db_files) cross-referenced with `code-program-mapping.md`

Last updated: 2026-07-02 (Pass 564)

---

## Overview

- 870 forms with DFM file identified in menu system
- 726 forms have table data from program analysis
- 286 unique Btrieve tables referenced across all forms
- Source: `rwn_symbols.json` `db_files` field (Btrieve file handles opened per program)

Limitation: `db_files` records Btrieve file handles opened by name at startup — not every table accessed; some tables may be conditionally opened and absent.

### Universal Infrastructure Tables (opened by >85% of all forms)

These tables are boilerplate — opened by nearly every EvoERP program. They are filtered out of the per-module tables below to highlight module-specific data.

| Table | Forms | Purpose |
|-------|-------|---------|
| BKAPDESC | 699 | AP description/notes (universal) |
| BKAPVEND | 694 | AP vendor master (universal — every module may reference vendors) |
| BKARCUST | 697 | AR customer master (universal — every module references customers) |
| BKCMACCN | 690 | CM (contact manager) account cross-ref |
| BKICMSTR | 690 | IC item master (universal — every module may reference parts) |
| BKSYHELP | 712 | System help strings table |
| DBAHLPID | 716 | DBA/EVO Help system ID table (F1 help lookup) |
| ISDRILL | 703 | DrillDown cross-reference (memo/notes link) |
| ISIS | 679 | ISTS system/config table (session-level) |
| ISLINKS | 690 | ISTS hyperlink/attachment cross-ref |
| ISLOG | 705 | ISTS session/error log |
| LANGDICT | 697 | Language/translation dictionary (multi-language support) |
| MKAHIST | 722 | Activity history / audit log (every transaction records here) |
